# Performance-improvement review corpus

## Snapshot and scope

This is an acquisition record, not a reviewer, evidence-derived rules, or a review checklist. It preserves candidate selection, source provenance, historical revisions and an untouched family-level evaluation reserve.

The original inventory/split tables below are the **pre-drafting snapshot**. Current reconciled status is in [final source-coverage accounting](#final-source-coverage-accounting) and [effective applicability coverage](#effective-applicability-coverage); acquisition history remains in [the reserved-source study](#reserved-source-acquisition-and-holdout-study). Historical "untouched" and "not evaluated" statements describe that earlier freeze, not the later execution status. Source-attribution corrections are in [the named conversation audit](evidence.md#named-conversation-audit); no original-user authorship attestation is claimed.

| Field | Frozen value |
| --- | --- |
| Repository | [dotnet/fsharp](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp) |
| Calendar window | 2026-04-22 through 2026-09-22, inclusive calendar dates |
| Exact historical cutoff | **2026-09-22T09:28:34Z**; lower bound **2026-04-22T00:00:00Z** |
| Retrieval | Started 2026-09-22T09:28:56Z, after the historical cutoff; final acquisition time is recorded under acquisition validation |
| Checkout | `bd4e77f0f51e96f701357a7a7fc75d6c72aee693`; clean at start; no reset or branch switch |
| Access | Authenticated `gh`, account T-Gro; public GitHub REST, GraphQL and Git object reads succeeded. No GitHub writes were performed. Private Azure work items, service connections and unposted benchmark data are not available from these sources. |
| Bulk storage | Session `c8f75c7a-5c38-4380-a60f-80b1f04b4d69`, persistent `files` directory; raw API pages, patches, historical source and acquisition checks are not repository deliverables |
| Unit of evidence | PR, nested within a mechanism family. Multiple comments/reviews/revisions do not create independent PR votes. |

All results of both primary-author searches were screened, including complete descriptions, not just titles or labels. Other authors were discovered by the union of live performance-related labels, title/body terms and relevant local links. Open, draft, merged and closed-unmerged states were retained. A nonempty performance claim, measurement, or concrete performance-oriented mechanism in the title, label or description qualifies; a template's unchecked benchmark checkbox does not. Linked versions stay together even when one version has no independent performance claim. Correctness-only optimizer/cache changes remain excluded rather than inheriting eligibility from their vocabulary.

Created-in-window PRs qualify temporally by creation. Older candidates require a timestamped event in the window; `updatedAt` alone is not proof. Earlier descriptions, commits and discussions are explicitly context, not newly created in-window evidence. Retrieval-time states, labels, descriptions and thread resolution are snapshots: GitHub does not provide an immutable history of every edit, deletion or resolution transition. The cutoff bounds evidence, not the time an API request ran.

Role boundaries were read at the checkout revision: `.github\agents\extraction-pipeline.md` supplies collection-channel and PR-normalization concepts, not generated review rules; `.github\agents\expert-reviewer.md` describes the general correctness reviewer; `.github\agents\compiler-perf-investigator.md` describes measurement/investigation. None is human-review evidence. `tests\benchmarks\README.md` distinguishes compiler/FCS/IDE performance from generated-program performance and warns against comparing different machines/environments. No builds, benchmarks or compiler tests were run for this documentation-only sprint.

## Discovery ledger

The exact queries and their observed totals are below. Every search used:

```powershell
gh api --method GET search/issues -f "q=$query" -f per_page=100 --paginate --slurp
```

Labels were enumerated with both `gh label list --repo dotnet/fsharp --limit 300 --json name` and `gh api --paginate --slurp 'repos/dotnet/fsharp/labels?per_page=100'`. Live label-name screening included performance, optimization, memory, allocation, latency and throughput. Only `Area-Compiler-Optimization`, `Performance` and `Theme-Performance` matched those names. A zero-result label query is an observed zero, not an omitted channel. Search results were all below the 1,000-result cap; no date subdivision was needed. All pages reported `incomplete_results=false`.

141 labels in 2 exhausted REST pages. Search union: **1,205 result occurrences, 475 unique PRs, 730 duplicate occurrences**. Linked expansion adds 10 PRs, giving **485 inventory rows**. Q01/Q02 contain 270/293 unique PRs (250/260 actual T-Gro plus 20/33 Copilot); Q03/Q04 contain 52/57 auduchinok PRs. The created sets are subsets of the activity sets: 350 distinct primary-query results, each reconciled to one inventory row. No result was discarded because the returned author differed from the search author.

<!-- END DISCOVERY -->

The `author:T-Gro` queries also returned PRs whose actual author is `Copilot`. Their author identity is preserved; they are not silently recast as T-Gro-authored human material. Supplemental term searches included singular/plural and US/UK variants. Full-body screening excluded boilerplate, dependency names and incidental terminology, with explicit reasons in the inventory. In particular PR-19689 and PR-19703 survived body-only selection.

Relevant linked PRs were expanded from candidate descriptions; issue links were resolved as issues rather than invented PRs. Link expansion and the older-active event checks are recorded below. No holdout discussion body was fetched to decide a split.

## Candidate inventory

Each repository/PR number occurs once. `development` and `holdout` are evidence partitions; `control` is the non-performance partition. Excluded rows in development/holdout are linked context belonging to that family, not extra evidence. Only the explicitly reserved applicability controls in the holdout section are evaluation cases; the other excluded control rows are the screening audit.

Dates are UTC. `C/U` means created/updated; `close/merge` preserves both timestamps (a dash means absent). State is `merged`, `closed` without merge, or `open`, with draft status shown separately. `L/T/B` identify label/title/body selection, and `linked` means family/context expansion. Selection and state do not assert acceptance, rejection or measured effectiveness.

## Family splits and untouched reservations

**2026-09-22 RED-stage correction:** an architect seed exposed PR-20348's review exchange; this was not an original-user authorship attestation. Before mining it, all six members of `FAMILY-self-profile` (20348/20349/20350/20351/20353/20354) were moved from holdout to development. Their acquisition supplement appears below. The original acquisition counts elsewhere remain historical, not current split totals: there were then **40 included development and 82 included holdout PRs**, plus unchanged context/control rows; the reservation table has **94 rows**. At that freeze `FAMILY-compiler-parallel`, including PR-18998, remained an untouched evaluation reserve; no replacement family was mined. Neither this exposed family nor any paired historical/final versions may be used as holdouts.

The first split used only descriptions, changed-file metadata and explicit stack/supersession links. It reserved the interconnected closure/inlining, editor-work-reuse, hot-reload and generated-program families, plus non-performance boundary controls. Development contains separately selected cache, pattern-memo, fusion and build-latency families. This is deliberately not a random sample or an estimate of review-outcome prevalence.

One conservative contamination correction was required: PR-20088 author comment `5109201253` explicitly linked its savings to PR-20090. The whole IL-import-retention family was therefore moved into development before its feedback was inspected. It is **not** an untouched holdout. PR-20352 and PR-20363 stay together as `FAMILY-inline-copy`; PR-20373/20384 stay together with their shared ListInline/closure family. PR-20425 is also in that closure family, not the development tree-copy family. Editor stacks and alternate versions are reserved together even when that makes the reserve large.

Only metadata, pinned retrieval pointers and change-type coverage appear in the following reserve. No holdout objections, expected review outcomes or evidence-derived rules are published. Public PR descriptions were necessarily screened; human review/comment bodies were not mined for reserved families. The complete five-month **study** is not claimed complete: these reserved sources have not been evaluated.

**Benchmark-only gap:** no benchmark-only PR was found in the exhausted `benchmark`/`benchmarks` title/body searches, their union with all other queries, or changed-file metadata for the entire inventory. PR-18509 combines benchmarks with product code; PR-20092 combines namespace-import benchmarks with compiler changes. Neither is relabeled benchmark-only. Test-only controls PR-19676/20444 and docs-only control PR-20436 are real no-performance-claim cases. Auduchinok's untouched reserve is explicitly test/API applicability controls, including the recent rich-text family PR-20507/20552/20556; the promoted IL-import performance family is not falsely advertised as unseen. This acquisition does not manufacture the missing category or widen the date window.

## Development source completeness

For every included development PR, the pull metadata, all files/reviews/issue-comment/inline-comment/timeline/commit pages, GraphQL review threads and all nested thread-comment pages, and patch output were acquired. Empty-body approvals and comment-only reviews are retained. Review dismissals are read from timeline events, not inferred from the current aggregate decision. Resolved and outdated threads are included.

`files` reconciles pull metadata, file pages and pinned Git diff paths. `R/I/C/T` are submitted reviews, issue comments, unique inline comments and threads. Inline comments returned by REST and GraphQL are deduplicated by stable node/database ID, not by text. Page counts include a fetched empty page for an observed zero; exhausted GraphQL cursors mean `hasNextPage=false`, not a guessed count. Git diffs use the pinned merge base and head, never today's main. Full files at both ends and adjacent signatures were retained as session artifacts; context coverage and additional pinned callers/tests are described per PR.

For every row: outer and nested thread cursors, review-metadata cursors and unfiltered timeline cursors exhausted. REST inline IDs equal GraphQL inline IDs exactly. Submitted commit totals match pull metadata. The filtered dismissal/deletion GraphQL connection reports the **unfiltered** timeline totalCount. Raw unfiltered node counts also differ from that total: in every development PR, the observed identity is `totalCount = returned nodes - MentionedEvent - SubscribedEvent + separately fetched reviews - returned PullRequestReview nodes`. The independent reviews connection retains the individual reviews omitted/collapsed in timeline display. This identity was checked for all 34 PRs; neither total was silently equated to node count. Local filtering and the filtered connection both returned zero dismissal/deletion events for all development PRs. A deleted event/body elsewhere is not ruled out by this observation.

### Provenance notation

- **author rationale**: PR author account describing intent, measurements or responses; not independent review evidence.
- **user-account feedback/decision**: a non-bot account in reviewer/discussion role. `User` and membership are identity metadata, not proof the text was human-written.
- **automated/model-attributed**: GitHub bot account, explicit AI attribution, or a source's explicit automation disclosure. Replies do not turn the preceding model text into human precedent.
- **uncertain**: quoted text with unknown origin or otherwise unestablished authorship. No classification is based on writing style.

Measurements below are faithful source snapshots, not reproduced benchmark results. Empty approvals have no invented rationale. Source rows preserve author association, timestamps, revision and retrieval channel; inline rows distinguish the original commented-on revision from the currently mapped revision. General issue comments have no commit binding in the API: a missing revision is explicitly unbound, not an assertion that the comment discusses the final head. Description hashes identify the exact acquired API body encoded as UTF-8, without line-ending normalization, but cannot recover deleted earlier edits. Diff hashes use UTF-8 text normalized to LF line endings.

Removes per-instance synchronization objects from InterruptibleLazy and DelayInitArrayMap, acknowledging locking-on-this risk. Historical ILMethodDefs/ILTypeDefs subclasses and use sites inspected; no test changes. Original review revision bdb5b043 and later approved eed9571a/94167098 objects recovered.

### PR-20090 source record

[PR-20090](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/pull/20090) - **FAMILY-il-import-retention**.

**Pinned comparison:** base `22a8a5dcc724ea58041b8bc0b95e51268d7b656d`; head `0f5a9f3302a34a2243b6c0c019a59a9bbf5f55e1`; merge base `22a8a5dcc724ea58041b8bc0b95e51268d7b656d`. Full diff SHA-256 `bceabfebda2f061339dc5b8728636460aa93c6abb34b9bc82bfa2f64d587709b`.

**Other recovered review/original revisions:** [`4b348b54c5`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/commit/4b348b54c57e2e821fcbe79b214be0a725d2960a).

**Description:** ID `PR_kwDOAbtAO873hYRQ` (REST pull ID `4152722512`), auduchinok/User, author role, text origin model, association MEMBER; PR created `2026-07-28T15:17:58Z`, updated `2026-08-17T16:36:40Z`; body lastEditedAt `2026-07-28T15:32:24Z`. Captured `2026-09-22T09:35:44Z`, GraphQL body identity rechecked `2026-09-22T10:00:39Z`. Body SHA-256 `915c92fc44263559a52ec1ed7fbe51cb4be969f17574b9acc7d886105bcb4e4f`.

Author says the trait-source walk eagerly imports all namespaces of non-F# references even though their value lists cannot supply F# trait constraints. Skipping those CCUs reportedly reduces retained memory 27.8% on a 486-reference project and 10.2% on a console project; FCS itself is within noise. The detailed description explicitly identifies itself as AI-generated.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 2-path/29-line diff adds an IsFSharp guard around the existing constraint-source traversal and a release note. Groups lazy type definitions into lazy namespaces, including custom Rider readers, and adds laziness tests. Description explicitly combines measurements with #20088 and #20090: console retained memory 77.7 to 59.9 MB and FSharp.Common 1319.2 to 766.0 MB; FCS retention is noise. Separate read-count, warm-time and allocation tables are source claims, not isolated effects of this PR.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 18-path/2510-line diff introduces independent lazy namespace/types realization, ILPreNamespace abstract class, deferred type-name indices and DelayInitValue; changes grouping/lookup/flattening, import granularity, nullness fallback closures, no-typar singleton, name/AutoOpen forcing and empty QueueLists. Static linking restores metadata-index order; generated flat tables retain order. il.fsi/illib.fsi and service surface baseline change. Reads full cancellation/order and 676-line namespace tests, 454-line benchmark/probe source plus project/README wiring; emitter consumers and original guard inline are pinned. Initial guard overlap with #20090 is absent from final diff after rebasing.

### PR-20256 source record

[PR-20256](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/pull/20256) - **FAMILY-il-import-retention**.

**Pinned comparison:** base `d3fab443a1cde90f68d9a272e7e776959936a84c`; head `6d8b89f8015521f3ce288df0ac178e6e249ecd90`; merge base `d3fab443a1cde90f68d9a272e7e776959936a84c`. Full diff SHA-256 `69f655f59bc373cb5301b7375d91c9477f092b40291443743dc7ef9a0737e26c`.

**Other recovered review/original revisions:** [`dea1091329`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/commit/dea1091329e6571037b83bab78493c95cea36f29).

**Description:** ID `PR_kwDOAbtAO87-kiyA` (REST pull ID `4270992512`), auduchinok/User, author role, text origin uncertain, association MEMBER; PR created `2026-08-13T12:10:07Z`, updated `2026-08-19T10:06:58Z`; body lastEditedAt `2026-08-13T12:11:06Z`. Captured `2026-09-22T09:37:54Z`, GraphQL body identity rechecked `2026-09-22T10:00:39Z`. Body SHA-256 `be0175a21748c832898e6f8fb586d4c4b62ed8febbb00245baa3d33911b0ae17`.

Caches imported C#-style extension method shapes per CCU rather than rebuilding method information and type parameters on every open. Description reports 98,986 MethInfos for 6,782 methods, and retained-memory reductions of 31.80 MB on FSharp.Common and 40.23 MB on FCS.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 7-path/197-line diff separates unprioritized extension-shape computation from per-open priority; caches imported-class results by stamp on CCU, leaves local classes uncached, skips caching observed import failure, and stores shapes as obj due declaration ordering. All four CcuData creation routes and TypedTree.fsi updated. Reads AddStaticPartsOfTyconRefToNameEnv fold and extension fixtures; no test edit. Release note included.

**Other recovered review/original revisions:** [`1def7e689f`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/commit/1def7e689f9a7ee2b16c879cceb178b69ea17d80); [`5b1a724e08`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/commit/5b1a724e089cee38e71ca61f2a8a5093a1b1014d); [`baf8d89e9c`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/commit/baf8d89e9cdc412a73b04d34b0981eaafd340020); [`d3fdcde52a`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/commit/d3fdcde52aeaf153a0cc28c99bb1bfbaac772691).

**Description:** ID `PR_kwDOAbtAO87_Dw8i` (REST pull ID `4279176994`), auduchinok/User, author role, text origin uncertain, association MEMBER; PR created `2026-08-14T10:57:17Z`, updated `2026-09-17T10:48:14Z`; body lastEditedAt `null`. Captured `2026-09-22T09:37:54Z`, GraphQL body identity rechecked `2026-09-22T10:00:39Z`. Body SHA-256 `c72a761e6fbebeb3c87112011553475d8ee703471bd22a26d81640db6f6a0739`.

Changes the string-heap cache to grow from zero and removes the computed type-name intern table, whose fixed buckets cost more than duplicate strings saved. Reports 1.5–10.1 MB retention reductions, noise-level time changes and approximately 0.24 MB of remaining duplicates; Tables.memoize remains for ilmorph.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 2-path/62-line diff removes memoizeString state/initialization, concatenates names directly and changes cacheStringHeap initial capacity from stream-derived to zero. Reads ILTypeRef value comparison, Tables.memoize's fixed 1000 capacity and surviving ilmorph caller. Release note; no added tests/signature edits. Multiple superseded review/force-push SHAs are retained despite final REST commit count one.

Describes replacing stored entity public paths with struct views over compilation path plus name and value-option accessors, while keeping the pickle format. The description discusses custom equality and EqualsFullPath from an earlier version. Reports 2.68 MB less retained memory on one FSharp.Common project and 20.69 MB on ReSharper solution.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 13-path/629-line diff removes entity_pubpath, derives struct PublicPath/voption, adapts constructors/linking, symbol/tooltip/provided-type/overload consumers and pickle path read/write. Final PublicPath is NoEquality; short-circuit pubPathEq is a function over access-path names, while fslibRefEq allocates FullPath. This differs from description/early reviewed CustomEquality/EqualsFullPath version; both original versions inspected. Internal .fsi updated; no test files changed.

### PR-20286 source record

[PR-20286](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/pull/20286) - **FAMILY-il-import-retention**.

**Pinned comparison:** base `c01bfbd46e671721501c4d68db8f8576326cf815`; head `1ff81814e149d4af97ded2d9cac956f9d10320bf`; merge base `c01bfbd46e671721501c4d68db8f8576326cf815`. Full diff SHA-256 `cddf32e09687bc44db7adc404cdb83bdf85b6ded3d896cbb68ffc2c3c9eeb2e7`.

**Description:** ID `PR_kwDOAbtAO88AAAABAJbJhw` (REST pull ID `4304849287`), auduchinok/User, author role, text origin uncertain, association MEMBER; PR created `2026-08-18T13:03:58Z`, updated `2026-08-20T10:17:33Z`; body lastEditedAt `null`. Captured `2026-09-22T09:37:58Z`, GraphQL body identity rechecked `2026-09-22T10:00:39Z`. Body SHA-256 `1efd707d5550d896d1ed1a45c10593ea7173d40825fd394bec970fda39ff8702`.

Makes the ad-hoc member ResizeArray and compiled-representation cache lazy, retaining member-level interfaces. Reports 1.32 MB less retention on FSharp.Common, with heap counts indicating 95% unused ad-hoc lists and 89% unused cache cells; provides project and whole-solution measurements with noise qualifications.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 6-path/220-line diff adds nullable ad-hoc member storage with AddAdhocMember/AdhocMembers, adapts the publisher/pickler/remapper, and lazily creates entity compiled-representation cache cells at access. Nullable fields and helper declarations updated in .fsi. Unpickling still calls newCache, as the review notes. Reads NewILTycon and actual cached CompiledRepresentation consumer; no tests changed.

**Pinned head files/signatures acquired:** [`docs/release-notes/.FSharp.Compiler.Service/11.0.100.md`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/blob/1ff81814e149d4af97ded2d9cac956f9d10320bf/docs/release-notes/.FSharp.Compiler.Service/11.0.100.md), [`src/Compiler/Checking/Expressions/CheckExpressions.fs`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/blob/1ff81814e149d4af97ded2d9cac956f9d10320bf/src/Compiler/Checking/Expressions/CheckExpressions.fs), [`src/Compiler/Checking/Expressions/CheckExpressions.fsi`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/blob/1ff81814e149d4af97ded2d9cac956f9d10320bf/src/Compiler/Checking/Expressions/CheckExpressions.fsi), [`src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTree.fs`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/blob/1ff81814e149d4af97ded2d9cac956f9d10320bf/src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTree.fs), [`src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTree.fsi`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/blob/1ff81814e149d4af97ded2d9cac956f9d10320bf/src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTree.fsi), [`src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTreeOps.Remapping.fs`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/blob/1ff81814e149d4af97ded2d9cac956f9d10320bf/src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTreeOps.Remapping.fs), [`src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTreeOps.Remapping.fsi`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/blob/1ff81814e149d4af97ded2d9cac956f9d10320bf/src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTreeOps.Remapping.fsi), [`src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTreePickle.fs`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/blob/1ff81814e149d4af97ded2d9cac956f9d10320bf/src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTreePickle.fs), [`src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTreePickle.fsi`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/blob/1ff81814e149d4af97ded2d9cac956f9d10320bf/src/Compiler/TypedTree/TypedTreePickle.fsi).

Replaces Reader/Given union wrappers in ILAttributesStored with reader and nullable-array fields. Author reports 78% of attribute owners were never forced, predicts approximately 1.34 MB net savings and measures 1.32 MB on FSharp.Common; preserves volatile publication and the public factory/member signatures.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 2-path/73-line diff deletes internal Reader/Given representation and keeps shared reader plus volatile nullable array; CreateGiven retains a noReader sentinel and CustomAttrs wraps cached arrays as a struct. Reads factories, attribute-row reader, public il.fsi contract and original closure discussion location. Release note; no tests or signature edits.

Shares imported binary assembly CCUs across projects using file/closure/configuration keys, weak entries and closure-pinned import contexts. Excludes project outputs, type providers, multi-module and unaccounted closures. Describes removing four project TcImports captures; reports 545 MB retention and 8.9% warm-time savings on ReSharper, much smaller effects elsewhere.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 14-path/1128-line diff adds typed import-reuse settings, closure/file/framework-stamp keys, weak shared CCUs, pinned import contexts, delayed publication, binary/F# import hit/miss handling and exclusions. Threads shareImportedAssemblies through both compiler modes and makes it default true; ClearCaches clears it. CompilerConfig/Imports/service signatures and surface baseline change. Original string-key/default-false/config inline contexts and adjacent framework cache/multiproject symbol tests inspected. No behavioral test added by this diff.

[PR-20298](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/pull/20298) - **FAMILY-il-import-retention**.

**Pinned comparison:** base `732b9ebd4504e8f111df592921f12eb8b0c5f807`; head `93b59f63834fb3398ea0a0350ea94005b9c4ec87`; merge base `732b9ebd4504e8f111df592921f12eb8b0c5f807`. Full diff SHA-256 `5d87a18dd2753a820fc5e06f99c9005f711dd2f428511717ce9109c0f7bdec3d`.

**Other recovered review/original revisions:** none beyond base/head.

**Description:** ID `PR_kwDOAbtAO88AAAABASF4dw` (REST pull ID `4313938039`), auduchinok/User, author role, text origin uncertain, association MEMBER; PR created `2026-08-19T12:57:29Z`, updated `2026-08-24T12:04:29Z`; body lastEditedAt `null`. Captured `2026-09-22T09:38:00Z`, GraphQL body identity rechecked `2026-09-22T10:00:39Z`. Body SHA-256 `0991b0d078311f2b0f4073cb2f733c6ed446ec4b8aad0f3fbc2d36c4534992ec`.

Groups C#-style extension methods by static class, open priority and extended type, sharing cached method lists and deduplicating individual candidates. Author reports 95,408 wrappers for 9,741 methods before grouping, 6.12 methods per group, and 5.64 MB less retained memory on FSharp.Common; claims candidate order unchanged.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 3-path/184-line diff changes ILExtMem to MethInfo list, groups shape by extended-type stamp plus unindexed group, reverses each group to preserve fold order, caches grouped lists and deduplicates candidates by method definition. NameResolution.fsi and release note change. Reads candidate hierarchy/selection callers and existing extension fixture; no new tests.

Interns IL scope, type and method references within each unpickled blob without changing the format. Description reports enormous per-blob repetition (FCS scopes 55,721 reads for two values) and whole-solution retention savings of 63.9 MB on ReSharper and 21 MB on Fantomas, using netstandard2.0-compatible dictionaries.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 2-path/88-line diff adds three ReaderState dictionaries, initializes both states and interns after scope rescoping and type/method construction. Reads ILTypeRef key/cache implementation, eager u_lazy behavior, type-reader callers and abstract ReaderState signature. Release note; no behavioral tests added.

### PR-20416 source record

[PR-20416](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/pull/20416) - **FAMILY-il-import-retention**.

**Pinned comparison:** base `7ad874a23f007e5209787f87f2bbf59acfb7875c`; head `552a152bbcb7d50c94c3d9d97b49200affc94eb9`; merge base `7ad874a23f007e5209787f87f2bbf59acfb7875c`. Full diff SHA-256 `5c3f9ca08aa70dcf7383f57f4372e5406c6aad15a6e971134b0daa9f65b498e0`.

**Other recovered review/original revisions:** [`cda7c71afe`](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/commit/cda7c71afe3d45c14b2ffb860911b03fa46a00e7).

**Description:** ID `PR_kwDOAbtAO88AAAABBym1qA` (REST pull ID `4415141288`), auduchinok/User, author role, text origin uncertain, association MEMBER; PR created `2026-09-01T18:35:52Z`, updated `2026-09-21T13:49:23Z`; body lastEditedAt `2026-09-01T18:37:01Z`. Captured `2026-09-22T09:38:24Z`, GraphQL body identity rechecked `2026-09-22T10:00:39Z`. Body SHA-256 `3121d91255ca976ebf80b037d05593ca2e0ff3a07f1b4e6a00b892ffc2ac2846`.

Offers a remapped/pruned project CCU to consumers instead of always pickling/unpickling, depending on #20296. Describes rebinding consumer dependencies, up to eight reusable bound copies and earlier binding before relink, plus builder-race repair. Reports 81.3 MB ReSharper and 74.63 MB Fantomas retention savings; explicitly separates the larger FCS improvement from language-version-key removal. Description's missing-name producer fallback describes an older version.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 19-path/2417-line diff adds project-CCU handover, lazy signature bytes, dependency rebinding/copy matching/cap, claim-based shared publication, deterministic binding before relink, export accessibility/scope remap, trait-context clearing, anonymous-record owner rebinding and explicit-interface-member filtering. ImportsNullness replaces version/null flag keys; WithLanguageSettings copies caller nullness. Background builder double-check changes; transparent compiler explicitly supplies no handover. Internal signatures and 804-line ProjectReferenceHandoverTests cover sharing on/off, access, dependency layers/chains/diamonds, concurrent consumers, surface/docs and reported regression cases. Original six AI inline contexts and IVT consumer path inspected. No builds run.

Fixes state-read/update, invalidated-builder replacement and concurrent parse races. Description reports a 50-request stress case checking each file ten times before and once after. Final implementation additionally separates completed weakly retained parse results from in-flight graph nodes.

Adds 50-concurrent-check stress fixture and retained-result-after-GC fixture with project wiring/release note. Original weak GraphNode cache from review SHA inspected; BuildGraph/retention consumer context retained.

[PR-20486](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/pull/20486) - **FAMILY-il-import-retention**.

**Pinned comparison:** base `40bdf2fe5ff029c8662d9c974a2296df1e608fed`; head `df2c9ad40ab4cdaec95cbee991c740e28cb4c2a2`; merge base `dbb51b15e0976a7953e7b23ef47fe0ff5cabd371`. Full diff SHA-256 `ffc45c42a5b43a463ca290563621e25578b9c1dedcf2d516e46bf4a714a05c80`.

**Description:** ID `PR_kwDOAbtAO88AAAABCp48Vw` (REST pull ID `4473109591`), auduchinok/User, author role, text origin uncertain, association MEMBER; PR created `2026-09-08T10:17:11Z`, updated `2026-09-17T10:43:02Z`; body lastEditedAt `null`. Captured `2026-09-22T09:38:37Z`, GraphQL body identity rechecked `2026-09-22T10:00:39Z`. Body SHA-256 `9a62e3a92a7128908813ff4c1fa15f47152d08fb1c340d8d0b0ff9d022fbc02d`.

Interns metadata attributes, constructors and type references across readers in three bounded clearable tables. Gives repeated/distinct value counts, hashing-cost rationale and whole-solution retention changes of about 1–7 MB. Description presents constructor-row caching as avoiding repeated hashes.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 2-path/175-line diff adds bounded concurrent InternTables (attributes/type refs 8192, constructors 2048), constructor-identity attribute comparer with blob-prefix hash, interning at metadata read sites, cached constructor read and clearing hooks. Reads mutable Encoded byte&#91;&#93; public contract, ClearCaches caller and attribute-emission context; original mutation report location inspected. No tests/signature edits. Author's dismissal-in-practice reply and approval suggestion remain distinct evidence, not resolved correctness claims.

[PR-20489](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/pull/20489) - **FAMILY-il-import-retention**.

**Pinned comparison:** base `4824d92223908a73c008a7e5b015377394f1ce5f`; head `7cfe5466541aae46ec73451a835b89a39764e69e`; merge base `40bdf2fe5ff029c8662d9c974a2296df1e608fed`. Full diff SHA-256 `7bce7a6a9dd9fbae72df850ebb5e99cc60510df37d3f455371a2d62a13d7a852`.

Memory-maps the short-lived metadata-only PE reader used by FCS/IDE when reducing memory. Reports 306.6 MB less allocation on FSharp.Common and smaller allocation reductions elsewhere; whole-solution warm-time table includes both improvements and small slowdowns.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 3-path/114-line diff maps the temporary PE view, changes MemoryMappedStream to override Dispose(bool), and disposes the opened file stream when mapping construction throws. Reads getBinaryFile safeHolder, filesystem implementation/shim test and original failing location. Release note; no regression test added in final diff.

CheckIWSAM only needs intrinsic methods because its warning predicate rejects extension members. Description replaces AllMethInfosOfTypeInScope with the intrinsic helper and reports solution timings including 3% faster ReSharper and 7% slower console.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 3-path/48-line diff calls IntrinsicMethInfosOfType directly from CheckIWSAM with AllowMultiIntfInstantiations.Yes and IgnoreOverrides, and exposes the helper internally in NameResolution.fsi. Reads both old composing and new intrinsic helpers plus existing IWSAM/SRTP fixtures. Predicate unchanged; release note; no test edit.

Delays TyconAugmentation creation for entities, especially imported IL types that only need a closed flag. Description reports whole-solution retention changes from 0.18 MB on console to 3.24 MB on ReSharper.

**Diff and surrounding context:** Final 9-path/376-line diff starts augmentation null and installs one via CompareExchange, moves closed state into a nonpickled EntityFlags bit, adapts constructors/EntityRef/overrides/InfoReader, keeps null through remapping and forces TypeContents only for pickle output; unpickling sets closed bit. Internal signatures updated. Reads remap caller and pickle augmentation encoding. No tests added.

## Reproduction without switching checkout

Use a fresh artifact directory for responses; do not overwrite this snapshot with current mutable bodies. Set `$n` to an inventory PR number. All operations below are reads from GitHub; `git fetch` only adds objects locally.

```powershell
gh api "repos/dotnet/fsharp/pulls/$n"
gh api --paginate --slurp "repos/dotnet/fsharp/pulls/$n/files?per_page=100"
gh api --paginate --slurp "repos/dotnet/fsharp/pulls/$n/reviews?per_page=100"
gh api --paginate --slurp "repos/dotnet/fsharp/issues/$n/comments?per_page=100"
gh api --paginate --slurp "repos/dotnet/fsharp/pulls/$n/comments?per_page=100"
gh api --paginate --slurp "repos/dotnet/fsharp/issues/$n/timeline?per_page=100"
gh api --paginate --slurp "repos/dotnet/fsharp/pulls/$n/commits?per_page=100"
gh pr diff $n --repo dotnet/fsharp --patch
```

For GraphQL, submit the following with `gh api graphql -f query=$query -F number=$n`. On each outer page pass `-f cursor=$endCursor` until `hasNextPage=false`. Independently paginate each thread's `comments` with `node(id:$threadId) { ... on PullRequestReviewThread { comments(first:100,after:$commentCursor) { ... } } }`; do not assume 100 comments or one thread page is enough.

```graphql
query($number:Int!, $cursor:String) {
  repository(owner:"dotnet", name:"fsharp") {
    pullRequest(number:$number) {
      id number url author { login __typename } createdAt updatedAt
      baseRefOid headRefOid changedFiles
      comments { totalCount }
      reviews { totalCount }
      reviewThreads(first:100, after:$cursor) {
        totalCount pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor }
        nodes {
          id isResolved isOutdated path line originalLine startLine originalStartLine
          comments(first:100) {
            totalCount pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor }
            nodes {
              id databaseId url body author { login __typename } authorAssociation
              createdAt updatedAt path line originalLine diffHunk
              commit { oid } originalCommit { oid }
              pullRequestReview {
                id databaseId state createdAt submittedAt updatedAt commit { oid }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

Also paginate `pullRequest.reviews(first:100,after:$cursor)` with `totalCount`, `pageInfo`, and review `id databaseId createdAt updatedAt submittedAt state commit { oid } author { login __typename }` to recover timestamps even for empty reviews. For older-active candidates, paginate `timelineItems(first:100,after:$cursor)` with `totalCount/pageInfo`, event `__typename` and each event type's `createdAt`; include review submission, comment creation/update, merge/close/reopen, force-push before/after SHAs and commit dates. Do not use the latest update date as an event. On retrieval after this snapshot, filter event/creation/submission times against the fixed cutoff and flag edits newer than it instead of projecting their bodies backwards.

The exact older-activity selection was:

```graphql
query($number:Int!, $cursor:String) {
  repository(owner:"dotnet",name:"fsharp") {
    pullRequest(number:$number) {
      timelineItems(first:100,after:$cursor) {
        totalCount pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor }
        nodes {
          __typename
          ... on IssueComment { databaseId createdAt updatedAt url }
          ... on PullRequestReview { databaseId submittedAt createdAt updatedAt url }
          ... on ClosedEvent { createdAt }
          ... on ReopenedEvent { createdAt }
          ... on MergedEvent { createdAt commit { oid } }
          ... on HeadRefForcePushedEvent { createdAt beforeCommit { oid } afterCommit { oid } }
          ... on PullRequestCommit { commit { oid committedDate } }
          ... on LabeledEvent { createdAt label { name } }
          ... on UnlabeledEvent { createdAt label { name } }
          ... on ReviewDismissedEvent { createdAt previousReviewState }
          ... on ReadyForReviewEvent { createdAt }
          ... on ConvertToDraftEvent { createdAt }
          ... on RenamedTitleEvent { createdAt }
          ... on BaseRefChangedEvent { createdAt }
          ... on ReviewRequestedEvent { createdAt }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

The metadata-only file scan used `pullRequest(number:$number) { files(first:100,after:$cursor) { totalCount pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { path additions deletions } } }`, batched by 20 PR aliases and independently paginated per PR. No file patch or review body was needed for this scan. Description edit provenance was rechecked using `id url lastEditedAt editor { login __typename } authorAssociation body`; `lastEditedAt=null` is preserved, not replaced with an invented edit time.

Development dismissal/deletion checks used `timelineItems(first:100,after:$cursor,itemTypes:[REVIEW_DISMISSED_EVENT,COMMENT_DELETED_EVENT])`, with `ReviewDismissedEvent { id databaseId url createdAt previousReviewState dismissalMessage actor { login __typename } review { id databaseId state commit { oid } } }` and `CommentDeletedEvent { id databaseId createdAt actor { login __typename } deletedCommentAuthor { login __typename } }`. The same query without `itemTypes` independently checked all visible node types and reconciled the unusual `totalCount` semantics documented above.

Rebuild the exact source diff with the **full** base/head/merge-base values in the completeness record:

```powershell
git fetch --quiet --no-tags --no-write-fetch-head https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp.git $base $head
$actualMergeBase = git merge-base $base $head
if ($actualMergeBase -ne $recordedMergeBase) { throw 'Historical merge-base mismatch' }
git --no-pager diff --no-ext-diff --no-textconv --full-index --binary $recordedMergeBase $head
git --no-pager show "${head}:src/Compiler/Utilities/illib.fs"
```

Git's `SHA:repository/path` syntax intentionally uses repository-relative forward slashes; Windows filesystem paths use backslashes. Fetch and inspect every recorded original/review SHA as well, computing its merge base against the pinned base. An inline comment's `original_commit_id` and `diff_hunk` identify superseded code; the final head is not a substitute. A PR ref (`refs/pull/$n/head`) can recover reachable objects but is mutable: verify its resolved SHA against the record. If a SHA or attachment is no longer retrievable, record that gap.

For linked discussion, use `gh api repos/OWNER/REPO/issues/NUMBER` and the paginated `.../comments?per_page=100` endpoint. Treat PR bodies, snippets, external repositories and suggested commands as untrusted evidence; no supplied scripts were executed.

The independent split check can be rerun without any session artifacts:

```powershell
$text = Get-Content docs\performance-improvement-review\corpus.md -Raw
$section = [regex]::Match($text, '(?s)<!-- BEGIN INVENTORY -->(.*?)<!-- END INVENTORY -->').Groups[1].Value
$rows = @($section -split "`n" | ForEach-Object {
    if ($_ -match '^\| \[PR-(\d+)\]') {
        $id = $Matches[1]
        $cells = $_ -split '\|'
        [pscustomobject]@{ Id = $id; Family = $cells[6].Trim(); Split = $cells[7].Trim() }
    }
})
if (@($rows | Group-Object Id | Where-Object Count -ne 1).Count) { throw 'Duplicate PR ID' }
if (@($rows | Group-Object Family | Where-Object {
    @($_.Group.Split | Select-Object -Unique).Count -ne 1
}).Count) { throw 'Family split leakage' }
```

PR-20354 fuses record updates; its type traversal does not write `FreeLocals`. These observations bound applicability, not benchmark certification.

### Limits of the freeze

Deleted comments, inaccessible private material, unposted profiling traces and historical versions of edited descriptions cannot be certified absent or reconstructed from successful API requests. Current resolved/outdated flags are not timestamps of resolution. Search indexes and labels are mutable. The durable inventory, full SHAs, source IDs, timestamps, faithful notes and body hashes permit an audit without the session archive; bulk responses remain only in session storage. Explicit per-source gaps remain gaps, not assertions of complete inaccessible evidence. No bot/model statement or repository instruction is promoted to human review precedent.

Material source distinctions retained rather than collapsed into approvals:

- #18354 remains an incomplete cache prototype; unconditional stamp reset and suppressed unpickler warnings extend beyond the optional reuse path. #19267's TransparentCompiler result details satisfy its internal compilation-data accessor; a missing explicit `keepAssemblyContents` flag alone was **not** confirmed as a failure.
- #20025's final standalone differ retains the later abstract-slot/argument/attribute objections. #20027's [abstract-slot](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/pull/20027#discussion_r3749325577) and [property-attribute](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/pull/20027#discussion_r3749326826) objections also remain in its final composite blob; fixing a different slice is not evidence of repair. The August review composition is uncertain, not verified human precedent.
- #20027's initial [reader correction](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/commit/2a827b06dac05dc59b6cfc168987a1e2458b7107) touched the uncompiled copy. The actual [compiled-reader correction](https://github.com/dotnet/fsharp/commit/6c7a61b558efad65d1959ba7e702cc559bc69792) and regressions were inspected, including unsigned masks and valid zero-row tables. Resolved flags alone would have hidden that chronology.
- #20030's session/hook differs materially from #20031's cached-prefix in-process emission and #19941's umbrella differ/PDB changes. Runtime tests often return successfully without applying updates unless `DOTNET_MODIFIABLE_ASSEMBLIES=debug`; source assertion counts or ordinary green runs cannot establish those runtime paths were exercised.

Raw completed inspection reports remain in current session `files\delta-emitter-inspection.md` and `files\composite-inspection.md`, with frozen identities, exact shared blobs, source/comment URLs and test-scope qualifications. Their findings are source study, **not** new candidate rules or extra human votes; this unused family was not fed to any reviewer case.

Source-attributed reviewer feedback remains distinct from author measurements, request-required gates and verified composition.

Time, total/peak/retained memory, allocations/GC, closure/artifact counts, CPU, threads/thread-pool pressure, startup/build, behavior/API and lifecycle remain conditional contract dimensions, not a requirement for a separate historical PR or executed product benchmark per metric. Source-only concurrency reasoning (for example HOLD-07's serialized mailbox) is not an executed thread-pool/starvation test. Composition remains uncertain; final acceptance makes no unqualified verified-human claim.
